Posts ONE PR-level comment with ✅ Implemented / ❌ Missing / 🔲 Not yet evaluated / 💬 Notes sections. Invoked by /kata-pr-review. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-acceptance.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-agent**: PR Reviewer. Senior-level code review on every diff before commit. Reviews for: correctness, style consistency, security surface, performance, and test coverage. Use for: /review (code quality lens), pre-commit review on any language or framework. ([thecoderbuddy/agile-team-skill](https://github.com/thecoderbuddy/agile-team-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-agent.md) · ★9)
- **pr-reviewer-consolidator**: Kata PR review consolidator. Runs LAST, after the parallel sub-reviewers. Fetches all &lt;!-- kata-review:* --&gt; inline + PR-level comments via gh api, deduplicates by {path, line} ±3, groups by severity, detects files with zero coverage from any inline agent, and posts ONE PR-level summary comment with metadata table, counts, highlights, and gap detection. Invoked by /kata-pr-review skill. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-consolidator.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-correctness**: Reviews a pull request diff for correctness defects - bugs, unhandled errors, edge cases, concurrency, data loss, and security. Reports only findings with a concrete failure scenario. Dispatched by the review-pr skill alongside pr-reviewer-design. Read-only - never edits files. ([konvoulgaris/soong](https://github.com/konvoulgaris/soong/blob/HEAD/plugins/soong/agents/pr-reviewer-correctness.md) · ★2)
- **pr-reviewer-design**: Reviews a pull request diff for contract and structural defects - API and schema breakage, misleading boundaries, and missing coverage on risky paths. Reports only findings with a concrete consequence. Dispatched by the review-pr skill alongside pr-reviewer-correctness. Read-only - never edits files. ([konvoulgaris/soong](https://github.com/konvoulgaris/soong/blob/HEAD/plugins/soong/agents/pr-reviewer-design.md) · ★2)
- **pr-reviewer-expert**: PR review agent crystallized from reverse-engineering CodeRabbit. Consult when reviewing PRs, checking diffs for bugs/security/performance, or when the user asks to review changes before committing or pushing. Trigger conditions: git diff output, PR descriptions, "review this", "check these changes", pre-push review, post-implementation quality check. - **pr-reviewer-instructions**: This file's text is included verbatim in the prompt of every commissioned PR reviewer. The instruments that commission reviewers read it at composition time and paste it in — never point at it, never paraphrase it. (Ruled 2026-08-30: one copy, included everywhere; a pointed-at rule gets skipped, a paraphrased one drifts.) ([nedschorus/nedschorus](https://github.com/nedschorus/nedschorus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/pr-reviewer-instructions.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-regression**: Kata PR sub-reviewer for AI-generated-code regressions and hallucinations. Catches unrelated deletions, phantom imports, wrong method signatures, weakened test assertions, dead code, TODOs in production, type-cast bypasses (the `any` ban from AGENTS.md is the headline rule), and silently swallowed errors. Kata code is largely AI-written so this is high-value. Invoked by /kata-pr-review. Posts inline comments via gh api. ([VicenzoMF/kata](https://github.com/VicenzoMF/kata/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer-regression.md) · ★1)
- **pr-reviewer-summary**: Kata PR sub-reviewer. Writes a TL;DR paragraph + "what this enables" bullets + (conditionally) a Mermaid diagram of new request flow, all posted as ONE PR-level comment (not inline). Invoked first by the /kata-pr-review skill so the consolidator can pin the summary at the top. Checks for App Tracking Transparency (ATT), PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy manifest, permission usage strings, Data Safety form alignment, privacy policy URL, account deletion mechanism, and sensitive data handling. Invoke this agent in parallel with other Review Ready auditors during a /review-ready:scan. ([manthanmk66/review-ready](https://github.com/manthanmk66/review-ready/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-auditor.md) · ★5)
- **privacy-auditor**: Audits code/diffs for anything that breaks Belong's "100% on-device, nothing leaves the box" thesis — new outbound network calls, data egress, secrets in tracked files, or telemetry. Use after AI/data/network changes and before committing. ([codename-dangerous-hackathon/project](https://github.com/codename-dangerous-hackathon/project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **privacy-auditor**: Reviews changes that touch analytics or logging for content leaks. Use when editing stats.py, the EVENTS tuple, any stats.track() call site, or logging of operator data. ([jimhoggey/RunsheetPilot-App](https://github.com/jimhoggey/RunsheetPilot-App/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **privacy-auditor**: Internal dynos-work agent. Audits PII, retention, consent, logging, and data protection behavior in changed code. Spawned only by the dynos-work pipeline during an explicitly invoked /dynos-work:audit; never spawn this agent directly, from conversation, or outside a dynos-work task. ([dynos-fit/dynos-work](https://github.com/dynos-fit/dynos-work/blob/HEAD/cli/assets/templates/base/agents/privacy-auditor.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **privacy-auditor**: Use when auditing an app for privacy / GDPR / CCPA / COPPA compliance — reviews data-processor inventory vs actual SDKs, cookie banners, privacy policy reachability, account-deletion flow, ASC + Play privacy form coverage, COPPA age gates, iOS ATT prompts. Autonomously files GitHub issues. ([mattsears18/shipyard](https://github.com/mattsears18/shipyard/blob/HEAD/plugins/shipyard/agents/privacy-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **privacy-auditor**: Data-flow review for DeclutrMail's no-body-storage privacy posture (D7, D228). Use on any PR touching Gmail data — apps/api/src/{gmail,senders,sync}/**, packages/workers/**, packages/shared/src/contracts/gmail-data-inventory.ts, packages/db/src/schema/{mail-messages,senders}.ts, anywhere calling Gmail API or Sentry/PostHog with message data. Reports findings; never refactors. ([CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail](https://github.com/CT2689-Tech/DeclutrMail/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-auditor.md))
- **privacy-compliance**: Privacy law compliance expert for GDPR, CCPA, HIPAA, and other regulations. Use PROACTIVELY when implementing privacy policies, consent management, or compliance workflows. ([bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public](https://github.com/bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-compliance.md) · ★3)
- **privacy-compliance-engineer**: Use for privacy engineering: data-subject-rights pipelines (access/erasure/portability) that locate and action data across systems, consent + lawful-basis tracking (granular, revocable, propagating), data minimization, automated retention/deletion, and correct pseudonymization-vs-anonymization. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/data-governance-privacy/agents/privacy-compliance-engineer.md) · ★7)
- **privacy-compliance-reviewer**: Use this agent when reviewing an Android app's privacy compliance including Data Safety Section accuracy, dangerous permission justification, data collection practices, GDPR/COPPA compliance, or consent mechanisms for Google Play Store. Examples: ([crgeee/google-playstore-toolkit](https://github.com/crgeee/google-playstore-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-compliance-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **privacy-compliance-reviewer**: Use this agent when reviewing an iOS app's privacy compliance including privacy manifests, Required Reason APIs, App Tracking Transparency, third-party SDK privacy requirements, or AI data sharing consent. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: A developer wants to ensure their app's privacy configuration is complete.\nuser: "Check if my app's privacy manifest and tracking setup is correct"\nassistant: "I'll use the privacy-compliance-reviewer agent to analyze your privacy manifest, Required Reason APIs, and tracking configuration."\n&lt;commentary&gt;\nThe user wants privacy compliance validation, which… ([crgeee/apple-appstore-toolkit](https://github.com/crgeee/apple-appstore-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-compliance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **privacy-doc-generator**: Draft and audit data-protection documents from a ROPA-driven model. TRIGGER WHEN: the user needs a Privacy Policy, Cookie Policy, DPA, consent notice, or DPIA under GDPR, ePrivacy, Codice Privacy, CCPA, LGPD, or FADP. DO NOT TRIGGER WHEN: general legal, contract, NDA, or IP questions (use legal-advisor); cookie banners, Consent Mode v2, or GTM (use digital-marketing:ga4-implementation-expert); business planning (use business-planner). ([acaprino/daodan](https://github.com/acaprino/daodan/blob/HEAD/exports/copilot/plugins/business/agents/privacy-doc-generator.agent.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **privacy-expert**: Use this agent for GDPR compliance, data protection, privacy policies, and cookie consent. This includes data retention, user consent management, and privacy by design implementation. ([oliverhees/aae-marketplace](https://github.com/oliverhees/aae-marketplace/blob/HEAD/saas-agency/agents/privacy-expert.md) · ★2)
- **privacy-expert**: Use for any task involving personal-data processing, data-protection compliance, or legal documents - GDPR/UK-GDPR controller obligations, the ICO Children's Code (AADC), PECR/cookies, the privacy notice or Terms of Use, DPIA upkeep, and sub-processor classification - AND for any third-party SDK or service integration that could collect, transmit, or log personal data (analytics, error monitoring, messaging, A/B testing), even when the issue's implementation ACs are detailed enough to skip the planner. Use BEFORE writing copy or code that changes how personal data is processed. Read-only. ([Frazzled-Productions/poke-memory](https://github.com/Frazzled-Productions/poke-memory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-expert.md) · ★1)
- **privacy-identity-steward**: Panel judge for privacy and identity. Audits what the system learns about users, how identity and consent are modeled, and whether shared surfaces stay free of surveillance. ([gbotev1/cc-autopilot](https://github.com/gbotev1/cc-autopilot/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-identity-steward.md) · ★5)
- **privacy-impact-assessment**: Detect PII exposure in a simpler-grants-gov PR diff and emit a draft HHS Privacy Impact Assessment covering data elements, purpose, retention, sharing, access controls, risks, and mitigations. ([btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation](https://github.com/btabaska/simpler-grants-documentation-automation/blob/HEAD/documentation/cursor-tooling/agents/privacy-impact-assessment.md) · ★1)
- **privacy-law-analyst**: You are a privacy law expert. You assess the scope of domestic and international privacy laws as they apply to a service's personal data processing activities, and derive specific obligations. ([chodolmu/taskforge-pro](https://github.com/chodolmu/taskforge-pro/blob/HEAD/harnesses/legal/privacy-engineer/agents/privacy-law-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **privacy-logging-reviewer**: Review collection, minimization, logging, telemetry, redaction, retention and privacy-sensitive data flows. Produce candidates only. ([omarmohelal/SecHelix](https://github.com/omarmohelal/SecHelix/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-logging.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **privacy-manifest-author**: Walks through filling in PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy by gap-checking actual API usage in source vs declared Required Reason API entries. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-mobile/agents/privacy-manifest-author.md) · 2 copies)

- **privacy-reviewer**: Optional reviewer for team-execution. Reviews implementations for privacy by design: data minimization, consent and purpose limitation, PII handling and classification, retention and deletion, and cross-border/compliance considerations. Triggered when plan contains: PII, GDPR, data classification, consent, retention, anonymize, personal data, privacy. NOT for: general security concerns (security-reviewer); legal determinations (flags for legal review). - **privacy-reviewer**: Semantic privacy scanner for committed-file diffs. Catches leaks the mechanical scripts/privacy_check.py misses, including contextual identity, financial, demographic, and personal-taxonomy disclosure. Independent voice; bound voices dual-invoke across two providers for genuine cross-provider double-guard. ([mhxie/Atelier](https://github.com/mhxie/Atelier/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **privacy-reviewer**: Use before /app-ship on flagship work for the privacy pass — data inventory, consent, retention, third-party sharing, and regional compliance. Distinct evidence set from security. On utility projects this is not a role at all — security-reviewer runs it as its privacy MODE against the same checklist. ([vmobifystudio/app-dev-team](https://github.com/vmobifystudio/app-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **privacy-reviewer**: Review collected fields, redaction, and export paths. Use when a collector, schema, or export change might leak identifying data. ([hoangsonww/Diffuse-Native-Apps](https://github.com/hoangsonww/Diffuse-Native-Apps/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **privacy-reviewer**: Read-only second pass over a drafted privacy redline. Use when the notice review has produced a redline and it needs checking before it goes to a human reviewer. ([product-on-purpose/agent-skills-toolkit](https://github.com/product-on-purpose/agent-skills-toolkit/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/anti/seeded-defects/privacy-notice-toolkit/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **privacy-reviewer**: Reviews changes for privacy, encryption, and data-protection regressions in Steps to Recovery. ([RipKDR/Steps-to-recovery-flutter](https://github.com/RipKDR/Steps-to-recovery-flutter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **privacy-reviewer**: Reviews PII handling, encryption correctness, data minimization, right-to-erasure, consent/lawful-basis, and PII-access logging. Runs after code-review approves, in parallel with security-reviewer and the other inner-loop reviewers. Iterates with the coder until clean. Does NOT cover correctness, exploitability/auth-bypass, reliability, telemetry, UI, accessibility, or infrastructure — those are handled by their dedicated reviewer agents. ([thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem](https://github.com/thurlow-research/HumanOversightSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/privacy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
